  95 |     |                 || cloneImplementation_ == address(0)
  96 |     |         ) revert ZeroAddress();
  97 |     |         governance = governance_;
  98 |     |         config = IProtocolConfigV1(config_);
  99 |     |         paymentToken = IERC20(IProtocolConfigV1(config_).paymentToken());
 100 |     |         emergencyController = IEmergencyControllerV1(emergencyController_);
 101 |     |         exposureGuard = ILaunchExposureGuardV1(exposureGuard_);
 102 |     |         bondEscrow = IBondEscrowV1(bondEscrow_);
 103 |     |         feeVault = IFeeVaultV1(feeVault_);
 104 |     |         fullMarketDeployer = IFullMarketDeployerV1(fullMarketDeployer_);
 105 |     |         cloneImplementation = cloneImplementation_;
 106 |     |         permit2 = permit2_;
 107 |     |     }
 108 |     | 
 109 |     |     modifier onlyGovernance() {
 110 |     |         if (msg.sender != governance) revert Unauthorized(msg.sender);
 111 |     |         _;
 112 |     |     }
 113 |     | 
 114 |     |     function createMarket(ProtocolTypes.CreateMarketParams calldata params, bytes32 userSalt)
 115 |     |         external
 116 |     |         nonReentrant
 117 |     |         returns (address market)
 118 |     |     {
 119 |     |         if (deprecated) revert InvalidConfiguration("factory.deprecated");
 120 |     |         if (emergencyController.isPaused(ProtocolTypes.PAUSE_MARKET_CREATION)) {
 121 |     |             revert PauseActive(ProtocolTypes.PAUSE_MARKET_CREATION);
 122 |     |         }
 123 |     |         ProtocolTypes.EconomicSnapshot memory economics = _validate(params);
 124 |     | 
 125 |     |         uint256 nonce = creatorNonce[msg.sender];
 126 |     |         creatorNonce[msg.sender] = nonce + 1;
 127 |     |         bytes32 salt = keccak256(
 128 |     |             abi.encode(
 129 |     |                 block.chainid,
 130 |     |                 address(this),
 131 |     |                 msg.sender,
 132 |     |                 nonce,
 133 |     |                 userSalt,
 134 |     |                 params.rulesHash,
 135 |     |                 params.closeAt,
 136 |     |                 params.marketPrimaryCap,
 137 |     |                 params.deploymentMode
 138 |     |             )
 139 |     |         );
 140 |     |         ProtocolTypes.MarketInitParams memory init = _buildInit(params, msg.sender, economics);
 141 |     | 
 142 |     |         if (params.deploymentMode == ProtocolTypes.DeploymentMode.FULL) {
 143 |     |             market = fullMarketDeployer.deploy(salt);
 144 |     |             IMarketVaultV1(market).initialize(init);
 145 |     |         } else {
 146 |     |             market = Clones.cloneDeterministic(cloneImplementation, salt);
 147 |     |             IMarketVaultV1(market).initialize(init);
 148 |     |         }
 149 |     | 
 150 |     |         if (isMarket[market]) revert MarketAlreadyRegistered(market);
 151 |     |         isMarket[market] = true;
 152 |     |         deploymentModeOf[market] = params.deploymentMode;
 153 |     |         deploymentSaltOf[market] = salt;
 154 |     |         exposureGuard.registerMarket(market);
 155 |     |         feeVault.registerAccruer(market);
 156 |     | 
 157 |     |         uint256 creationFee_ = config.creationFee();
 158 |     |         if (creationFee_ != 0) {
 159 |     |             _transferExact(msg.sender, address(feeVault), creationFee_);
 160 |     |             feeVault.accrue(
 161 |     |                 config.protocolTreasury(),
 162 |     |                 creationFee_,
 163 |     |                 FEE_KIND_CREATION,
 164 |     |                 bytes32(uint256(uint160(market)))
 165 |     |             );
 166 |     |         }
 167 |     |         _transferExact(msg.sender, address(bondEscrow), params.creatorBond);
 168 |     |         bondEscrow.lockBond(market, msg.sender, params.creatorBond);
 169 |     | 
 170 |     |         _emitMarketCreated(market, msg.sender, params, salt, nonce, creationFee_);
 171 |     |     }
 172 |     | 
 173 |     |     function _emitMarketCreated(
 174 |     |         address market,
 175 |     |         address creator_,
 176 |     |         ProtocolTypes.CreateMarketParams calldata params,
 177 |     |         bytes32 salt,
 178 |     |         uint256 nonce,
 179 |     |         uint256 creationFee_
 180 |     |     ) internal {
 181 |     |         address implementation = params.deploymentMode == ProtocolTypes.DeploymentMode.CLONE
 182 |     |             ? cloneImplementation
 183 |     |             : address(0);
 184 |     |         emit MarketCreated(
 185 |     |             market,
 186 |     |             creator_,
 187 |     |             params.deploymentMode,
 188 |     |             implementation,
 189 |     |             salt,
 190 |     |             market.codehash,
 191 |     |             nonce,
 192 |     |             creationFee_,
 193 |     |             params.creatorBond
 194 |     |         );
 195 |     |     }
 196 |     | 
 197 |     |     function setMarketplace(address marketplace_) external onlyGovernance {
 198 |     |         if (marketplace_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();
 199 |     |         if (marketplace != address(0)) revert AlreadyConfigured();
 200 |     |         marketplace = marketplace_;
 201 |     |         feeVault.registerAccruer(marketplace_);
 202 |     |         emit MarketplaceConfigured(marketplace_);
 203 |     |     }
 204 |     | 
 205 |     |     function setDeprecated(bool deprecated_) external onlyGovernance {
 206 |     |         deprecated = deprecated_;
 207 |     |         emit FactoryDeprecationUpdated(deprecated_);
 208 |     |     }
 209 |     | 
 210 |     |     function predictCloneAddress(
 211 |     |         address creator_,
 212 |     |         uint256 nonce,
 213 |     |         bytes32 userSalt,
 214 |     |         bytes32 rulesHash_,
 215 |     |         uint64 closeAt_,
 216 |     |         uint128 marketCap_
 217 |     |     ) external view returns (address predicted) {
 218 |     |         bytes32 salt = keccak256(
 219 |     |             abi.encode(
 220 |     |                 block.chainid,
 221 |     |                 address(this),
 222 |     |                 creator_,
 223 |     |                 nonce,
 224 |     |                 userSalt,
 225 |     |                 rulesHash_,
 226 |     |                 closeAt_,
 227 |     |                 marketCap_,
 228 |     |                 ProtocolTypes.DeploymentMode.CLONE
 229 |     |             )
 230 |     |         );
 231 |     |         return Clones.predictDeterministicAddress(cloneImplementation, salt, address(this));
 232 |     |     }
 233 |     | 
 234 |     |     function requiredBond(uint128 marketPrimaryCap) public pure returns (uint256) {
 235 |     |         return Math.max(
 236 |     |             MIN_CREATOR_BOND,
 237 |     |             Math.mulDiv(marketPrimaryCap, BOND_RATE_BPS, ProtocolTypes.BPS, Math.Rounding.Ceil)
 238 |     |         );
 239 |     |     }
 240 |     | 
 241 |     |     function _buildInit(
 242 |     |         ProtocolTypes.CreateMarketParams calldata params,
 243 |     |         address creator_,
 244 |     |         ProtocolTypes.EconomicSnapshot memory economics
 245 |     |     ) internal view returns (ProtocolTypes.MarketInitParams memory init) {
 246 |     |         init = ProtocolTypes.MarketInitParams({
 247 |     |             factory: address(this),
 248 |     |             paymentToken: address(paymentToken),
 249 |     |             config: address(config),
 250 |     |             emergencyController: address(emergencyController),
 251 |     |             exposureGuard: address(exposureGuard),
 252 |     |             bondEscrow: address(bondEscrow),
 253 |     |             feeVault: address(feeVault),
 254 |     |             permit2: permit2,
 255 |     |             creator: creator_,
 256 |     |             rulesHash: params.rulesHash,
 257 |     |             metadataURI: params.metadataURI,
 258 |     |             resolutionSourceHash: params.resolutionSourceHash,
 259 |     |             resolutionSourceURI: params.resolutionSourceURI,
 260 |     |             outcomeCount: params.outcomeCount,
 261 |     |             createdAt: block.timestamp.toUint64(),
 262 |     |             closeAt: params.closeAt,
 263 |     |             earlyBirdStart: params.earlyBirdStart,
 264 |     |             creatorTreasury: params.creatorTreasury,
 265 |     |             deploymentMode: params.deploymentMode,
 266 |     |             featureFlags: params.featureFlags,
 267 |     |             perUserPrimaryCap: params.perUserPrimaryCap,
 268 |     |             marketPrimaryCap: params.marketPrimaryCap,
 269 |     |             minimumPrimaryUnits: params.minimumPrimaryUnits,
 270 |     |             minimumC2CUnits: params.minimumC2CUnits,
 271 |     |             creatorBond: params.creatorBond,
 272 |     |             economics: economics
 273 |     |         });
 274 |     |     }
 275 |     | 
 276 |     |     function _validate(ProtocolTypes.CreateMarketParams calldata params)
 277 |     |         internal
 278 |     |         view
 279 |     |         returns (ProtocolTypes.EconomicSnapshot memory economics)
 280 |     |     {
 281 |     |         if (params.rulesHash == bytes32(0)) revert InvalidConfiguration("rulesHash");
 282 |     |         if (params.creatorTreasury == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();
 283 |     |         if (bytes(params.metadataURI).length > MAX_URI_LENGTH) {
 284 |     |             revert UriTooLong(bytes(params.metadataURI).length, MAX_URI_LENGTH);
 285 |     |         }
 286 |     |         if (bytes(params.resolutionSourceURI).length > MAX_URI_LENGTH) {
 287 |     |             revert UriTooLong(bytes(params.resolutionSourceURI).length, MAX_URI_LENGTH);
 288 |     |         }
 289 |     |         if (params.outcomeCount < 2 || params.outcomeCount > 32) {
 290 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("outcomeCount", params.outcomeCount, 2, 32);
 291 |     |         }
 292 |     |         if (
 293 |     |             params.closeAt < block.timestamp + 5 minutes
 294 |     |                 || params.closeAt > block.timestamp + 90 days
 295 |     |                 || params.earlyBirdStart < block.timestamp
 296 |     |                 || params.earlyBirdStart >= params.closeAt
 297 |     |         ) revert InvalidConfiguration("market.times");
 298 |     |         if ((params.featureFlags & ~SUPPORTED_FEATURE_FLAGS) != 0) {
 299 |     |             revert UnsupportedFeatureFlags(params.featureFlags);
 300 |     |         }
 301 |     | 
 302 |     |         uint256 modeCap = params.deploymentMode == ProtocolTypes.DeploymentMode.FULL
 303 |     |             ? config.maxFullMarketCap()
 304 |     |             : config.maxCloneMarketCap();
 305 |     |         if (params.marketPrimaryCap == 0 || params.marketPrimaryCap > modeCap) {
 306 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("marketPrimaryCap", params.marketPrimaryCap, 1, modeCap);
 307 |     |         }
 308 |     |         if (
 309 |     |             params.perUserPrimaryCap == 0
 310 |     |                 || params.perUserPrimaryCap > config.maxPerUserPrimaryCap()
 311 |     |                 || params.perUserPrimaryCap > params.marketPrimaryCap
 312 |     |         ) {
 313 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange(
 314 |     |                 "perUserPrimaryCap", params.perUserPrimaryCap, 1, config.maxPerUserPrimaryCap()
 315 |     |             );
 316 |     |         }
 317 |     |         _validateMinimum(
 318 |     |             "minimumPrimaryUnits", params.minimumPrimaryUnits, params.perUserPrimaryCap
 319 |     |         );
 320 |     |         _validateMinimum("minimumC2CUnits", params.minimumC2CUnits, params.marketPrimaryCap);
 321 |     | 
 322 |     |         uint256 minimumBond = requiredBond(params.marketPrimaryCap);
 323 |     |         if (params.creatorBond < minimumBond || params.creatorBond > MAX_CREATOR_BOND) {
 324 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("creatorBond", params.creatorBond, minimumBond, MAX_CREATOR_BOND);
 325 |     |         }
 326 |     |         economics = config.snapshot(params.creatorRakeBps, params.creatorC2CFeeBps);
 327 |     |     }
 328 |     | 
 329 |     |     function _validateMinimum(bytes32 field, uint128 value, uint128 cap) internal pure {
 330 |     |         if (value < MIN_CONFIGURED_UNITS || value > MAX_CONFIGURED_UNITS || value > cap) {
 331 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange(
 332 |     |                 field, value, MIN_CONFIGURED_UNITS, Math.min(MAX_CONFIGURED_UNITS, cap)
 333 |     |             );
 334 |     |         }
 335 |     |     }
 336 |     | 
 337 |     |     function _transferExact(address from, address to, uint256 amount) internal {
 338 |     |         uint256 beforeBalance = paymentToken.balanceOf(to);
 339 |     |         paymentToken.safeTransferFrom(from, to, amount);
 340 |     |         uint256 received = paymentToken.balanceOf(to) - beforeBalance;
 341 |     |         if (received != amount) revert InexactTokenTransfer(amount, received);
 342 |     |     }
 343 |     | }

  13 |     | /// @notice Timelocked protocol defaults and V1 hard economic bounds.
  14 |     | /// @dev Changes affect new markets only unless the field is explicitly protocol-level.
  15 |     | contract ProtocolConfigV1 {
  16 |     |     uint16 public constant HARD_MAX_CREATOR_RAKE_BPS = 1000;
  17 |     |     uint16 public constant HARD_MAX_PROTOCOL_SHARE_BPS = 5000;
  18 |     |     uint16 public constant HARD_MAX_EARLY_BIRD_SHARE_BPS = 5000;
  19 |     |     uint16 public constant HARD_MAX_C2C_FEE_BPS = 200;
  20 |     |     uint128 public constant HARD_MAX_FULL_MARKET_CAP = 5000e6;
  21 |     |     uint128 public constant HARD_MAX_CLONE_MARKET_CAP = 500e6;
  22 |     |     uint128 public constant HARD_MAX_PER_USER_PRIMARY_CAP = 100e6;
  23 |     |     uint128 public constant HARD_MAX_CREATION_FEE = 100e6;
  24 |     | 
  25 |     |     address public immutable governance;
  26 |     |     address public immutable paymentToken;
  27 |     | 
  28 |     |     address public protocolTreasury;
  29 |     |     uint128 public creationFee;
  30 |     |     uint16 public protocolShareBps;
  31 |     |     uint16 public earlyBirdShareBps;
  32 |     |     uint16 public platformC2CFeeBps;
  33 |     | 
  34 |     |     uint128 public maxFullMarketCap = HARD_MAX_FULL_MARKET_CAP;
  35 |     |     uint128 public maxCloneMarketCap = HARD_MAX_CLONE_MARKET_CAP;
  36 |     |     uint128 public maxPerUserPrimaryCap = HARD_MAX_PER_USER_PRIMARY_CAP;
  37 |     |     uint16 public maxCreatorRakeBps = HARD_MAX_CREATOR_RAKE_BPS;
  38 |     |     uint16 public maxCreatorC2CFeeBps = HARD_MAX_C2C_FEE_BPS;
  39 |     | 
  40 |     |     event ProtocolTreasuryUpdated(address indexed previousTreasury, address indexed newTreasury);
  41 |     |     event CreationFeeUpdated(uint256 previousFee, uint256 newFee);
  42 |     |     event ProtocolShareUpdated(uint16 previousBps, uint16 newBps);
  43 |     |     event EarlyBirdShareUpdated(uint16 previousBps, uint16 newBps);
  44 |     |     event PlatformC2CFeeUpdated(uint16 previousBps, uint16 newBps);
  45 |     |     event MarketCapLimitsUpdated(uint128 fullMarketCap, uint128 cloneMarketCap);
  46 |     |     event PerUserPrimaryCapLimitUpdated(uint128 previousCap, uint128 newCap);
  47 |     |     event CreatorFeeLimitsUpdated(uint16 creatorRakeBps, uint16 creatorC2CFeeBps);
  48 |     | 
  49 |     |     constructor(address governance_, address paymentToken_, address treasury_) {
  50 |     |         if (governance_ == address(0) || paymentToken_ == address(0) || treasury_ == address(0)) {
  51 |     |             revert ZeroAddress();
  52 |     |         }
  53 |     |         if (IERC20Metadata(paymentToken_).decimals() != 6) {
  54 |     |             revert InvalidConfiguration("paymentToken.decimals");
  55 |     |         }
  56 |     |         governance = governance_;
  57 |     |         paymentToken = paymentToken_;
  58 |     |         protocolTreasury = treasury_;
  59 |     |         earlyBirdShareBps = 2000;
  60 |     |     }

  63 |     |         if (msg.sender != governance) revert Unauthorized(msg.sender);
  64 |     |         _;
  65 |     |     }

  67 |     |     function setProtocolTreasury(address newTreasury) external onlyGovernance {
  68 |     |         if (newTreasury == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();
  69 |     |         address previous = protocolTreasury;
  70 |     |         protocolTreasury = newTreasury;
  71 |     |         emit ProtocolTreasuryUpdated(previous, newTreasury);
  72 |     |     }
  73 |     | 
  74 |     |     function setCreationFee(uint128 newFee) external onlyGovernance {
  75 |     |         if (newFee > HARD_MAX_CREATION_FEE) {
  76 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("creationFee", newFee, 0, HARD_MAX_CREATION_FEE);
  77 |     |         }
  78 |     |         uint128 previous = creationFee;
  79 |     |         creationFee = newFee;
  80 |     |         emit CreationFeeUpdated(previous, newFee);
  81 |     |     }
  82 |     | 
  83 |     |     function setProtocolShareBps(uint16 newBps) external onlyGovernance {
  84 |     |         if (newBps > HARD_MAX_PROTOCOL_SHARE_BPS) {
  85 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("protocolShareBps", newBps, 0, HARD_MAX_PROTOCOL_SHARE_BPS);
  86 |     |         }
  87 |     |         uint16 previous = protocolShareBps;
  88 |     |         protocolShareBps = newBps;
  89 |     |         emit ProtocolShareUpdated(previous, newBps);
  90 |     |     }
  91 |     |

  93 |     |         if (newBps > HARD_MAX_EARLY_BIRD_SHARE_BPS) {
  94 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("earlyBirdShareBps", newBps, 0, HARD_MAX_EARLY_BIRD_SHARE_BPS);
  95 |     |         }
  96 |     |         uint16 previous = earlyBirdShareBps;
  97 |     |         earlyBirdShareBps = newBps;

 103 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("platformC2CFeeBps", newBps, 0, HARD_MAX_C2C_FEE_BPS);
 104 |     |         }

 106 |     |         platformC2CFeeBps = newBps;

 110 |     |     /// @notice Sets limits applied only when subsequently creating a market.
 111 |     |     function setMarketCapLimits(uint128 fullCap, uint128 cloneCap) external onlyGovernance {
 112 |     |         if (fullCap > HARD_MAX_FULL_MARKET_CAP) {
 113 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("maxFullMarketCap", fullCap, 0, HARD_MAX_FULL_MARKET_CAP);
 114 |     |         }
 115 |     |         if (cloneCap > HARD_MAX_CLONE_MARKET_CAP) {
 116 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("maxCloneMarketCap", cloneCap, 0, HARD_MAX_CLONE_MARKET_CAP);
 117 |     |         }
 118 |     |         maxFullMarketCap = fullCap;
 119 |     |         maxCloneMarketCap = cloneCap;
 120 |     |         emit MarketCapLimitsUpdated(fullCap, cloneCap);
 121 |     |     }
 122 |     | 
 123 |     |     /// @notice Sets the per-user limit applied only when subsequently creating a market.
 124 |     |     function setMaxPerUserPrimaryCap(uint128 newCap) external onlyGovernance {
 125 |     |         if (newCap > HARD_MAX_PER_USER_PRIMARY_CAP) {
 126 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("maxPerUserPrimaryCap", newCap, 0, HARD_MAX_PER_USER_PRIMARY_CAP);
 127 |     |         }
 128 |     |         uint128 previous = maxPerUserPrimaryCap;
 129 |     |         maxPerUserPrimaryCap = newCap;
 130 |     |         emit PerUserPrimaryCapLimitUpdated(previous, newCap);
 131 |     |     }
 132 |     | 
 133 |     |     /// @notice Sets creator fee limits applied only when subsequently creating a market.
 134 |     |     function setCreatorFeeLimits(uint16 rakeBps, uint16 c2cFeeBps) external onlyGovernance {
 135 |     |         if (rakeBps > HARD_MAX_CREATOR_RAKE_BPS) {
 136 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("maxCreatorRakeBps", rakeBps, 0, HARD_MAX_CREATOR_RAKE_BPS);
 137 |     |         }
 138 |     |         if (c2cFeeBps > HARD_MAX_C2C_FEE_BPS) {
 139 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("maxCreatorC2CFeeBps", c2cFeeBps, 0, HARD_MAX_C2C_FEE_BPS);
 140 |     |         }
 141 |     |         maxCreatorRakeBps = rakeBps;
 142 |     |         maxCreatorC2CFeeBps = c2cFeeBps;
 143 |     |         emit CreatorFeeLimitsUpdated(rakeBps, c2cFeeBps);
 144 |     |     }
 145 |     | 
 146 |     |     function snapshot(uint16 creatorRakeBps, uint16 creatorC2CFeeBps)
 147 |     |         external
 148 |     |         view
 149 |     |         returns (ProtocolTypes.EconomicSnapshot memory result)
 150 |     |     {
 151 |     |         if (creatorRakeBps > maxCreatorRakeBps) {
 152 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("creatorRakeBps", creatorRakeBps, 0, maxCreatorRakeBps);
 153 |     |         }
 154 |     |         if (creatorC2CFeeBps > maxCreatorC2CFeeBps) {
 155 |     |             revert ValueOutOfRange("creatorC2CFeeBps", creatorC2CFeeBps, 0, maxCreatorC2CFeeBps);
 156 |     |         }
 157 |     |         result = ProtocolTypes.EconomicSnapshot({
 158 |     |             creatorRakeBps: creatorRakeBps,
 159 |     |             protocolShareBps: protocolShareBps,
 160 |     |             earlyBirdShareBps: earlyBirdShareBps,
 161 |     |             platformC2CFeeBps: platformC2CFeeBps,
 162 |     |             creatorC2CFeeBps: creatorC2CFeeBps,
 163 |     |             protocolTreasury: protocolTreasury
 164 |     |         });
 165 |     |     }

/Users/undef1ned/Work/local/cpredict/src/interfaces/IMarketVaultV1.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | pragma solidity 0.8.36;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import { IERC1155 } from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC1155/IERC1155.sol";
  5 |     | import { ProtocolTypes } from "../libraries/ProtocolTypes.sol";
  6 |     |

 238 |     |     /// @notice Conservative exposure used only by the launch guard.
 239 |     |     function guardExposure() external view returns (uint256) {
 240 |     |         ProtocolTypes.MarketState state = marketState;
 241 |     |         if (state == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.OPEN) return totalPrincipal;
 242 |     |         if (state == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.RESOLVED) {
 243 |     |             return remainingWinnerPool + remainingEarlyBirdPool;
 244 |     |         }
 245 |     |         return remainingRefundPrincipal + remainingTimeoutBonusPool;
 246 |     |     }

 259 | *   |                 || params.feeVault == address(0) || params.creator == address(0)

 267 | *   |         if (bytes(params.metadataURI).length > MAX_URI_LENGTH) {

 269 |     |         }

 335 |     |     /// @notice Updates non-economic market metadata before the first primary purchase.
 336 |     |     function updateBeforeFirstBuy(
 337 |     |         bytes32 newRulesHash,
 338 |     |         string calldata newMetadataURI,
 339 |     |         bytes32 newResolutionSourceHash,
 340 |     |         string calldata newResolutionSourceURI,
 341 |     |         uint64 newCloseAt,
 342 |     |         uint64 newEarlyBirdStart,
 343 |     |         address newCreatorTreasury,
 344 |     |         uint256 newFeatureFlags
 345 |     |     ) external onlyCreator {
 346 |     |         if (firstBuyOccurred) revert ImmutableAfterFirstBuy();
 347 | *   |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.OPEN) revert MarketTerminal();
 348 |     |         if (newRulesHash == bytes32(0)) revert InvalidConfiguration("rulesHash");
 349 |     |         if (newCreatorTreasury == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();
 350 |     |         if (bytes(newMetadataURI).length > MAX_URI_LENGTH) {
 351 |     |             revert UriTooLong(bytes(newMetadataURI).length, MAX_URI_LENGTH);
 352 |     |         }
 353 |     |         if (bytes(newResolutionSourceURI).length > MAX_URI_LENGTH) {
 354 |     |             revert UriTooLong(bytes(newResolutionSourceURI).length, MAX_URI_LENGTH);
 355 |     |         }
 356 |     |         if ((newFeatureFlags & ~SUPPORTED_FEATURE_FLAGS) != 0) {
 357 |     |             revert UnsupportedFeatureFlags(newFeatureFlags);
 358 |     |         }
 359 |     |         _validateTimes(createdAt, newCloseAt, newEarlyBirdStart);
 360 |     |         if (newCloseAt <= block.timestamp) revert MarketNotOpen();
 361 |     | 
 362 | *   |         rulesHash = newRulesHash;
 363 | *   |         resolutionSourceHash = newResolutionSourceHash;
 364 | *   |         resolutionSourceURI = newResolutionSourceURI;
 365 |     |         closeAt = newCloseAt;
 366 |     |         earlyBirdStart = newEarlyBirdStart;
 367 | *   |         creatorTreasury = newCreatorTreasury;
 368 | *   |         featureFlags = newFeatureFlags;
 369 |     |         _setURI(newMetadataURI);

 382 |     |     function buy(
 383 |     |         uint256 outcomeId,
 384 |     |         uint256 desiredUnits,
 385 |     |         uint256 minUnits,
 386 |     |         uint256 maxPayment,
 387 |     |         uint64 deadline
 388 |     |     ) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 filledUnits) {
 389 |     |         filledUnits = _prepareBuy(
 390 |     |             msg.sender, outcomeId, desiredUnits, minUnits, maxPayment, deadline
 391 |     |         );
 392 |     |         _pullExact(msg.sender, filledUnits);
 393 |     |         _completeBuy(msg.sender, outcomeId, desiredUnits, filledUnits);
 394 |     |     }
 395 |     | 
 396 |     |     function buyWithPermit2(
 397 |     |         address owner,
 398 |     |         uint256 outcomeId,
 399 |     |         uint256 desiredUnits,
 400 |     |         uint256 minUnits,
 401 |     |         uint256 maxPayment,
 402 |     |         uint64 callDeadline,
 403 |     |         ISignatureTransfer.PermitTransferFrom calldata permit,
 404 |     |         bytes calldata signature
 405 |     |     ) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 filledUnits) {
 406 |     |         if (!permit2Enabled() || address(permit2) == address(0)) {
 407 |     |             revert Permit2Disabled();
 408 |     |         }
 409 |     |         if (emergencyController.isPaused(ProtocolTypes.PAUSE_PERMIT2)) {
 410 |     |             revert PauseActive(ProtocolTypes.PAUSE_PERMIT2);
 411 |     |         }
 412 |     |         filledUnits =
 413 |     |             _prepareBuy(owner, outcomeId, desiredUnits, minUnits, maxPayment, callDeadline);
 414 |     |         if (
 415 |     |             permit.permitted.token != address(_paymentToken)
 416 |     |                 || permit.permitted.amount < filledUnits
 417 |     |         ) {
 418 |     |             revert InvalidConfiguration("permit2.permissions");
 419 |     |         }
 420 |     | 
 421 |     |         bytes32 witness = keccak256(
 422 |     |             abi.encode(
 423 |     |                 BUY_WITNESS_TYPEHASH,
 424 |     |                 owner,
 425 |     |                 address(this),
 426 |     |                 this.buyWithPermit2.selector,
 427 |     |                 outcomeId,
 428 |     |                 desiredUnits,
 429 |     |                 minUnits,
 430 |     |                 maxPayment,
 431 |     |                 callDeadline,
 432 |     |                 block.chainid
 433 |     |             )
 434 |     |         );
 435 |     |         _pullWithPermit2(owner, filledUnits, permit, witness, signature);
 436 |     |         _completeBuy(owner, outcomeId, desiredUnits, filledUnits);
 437 |     |     }
 438 |     | 
 439 |     |     function _pullWithPermit2(
 440 |     |         address owner,
 441 |     |         uint256 amount,
 442 |     |         ISignatureTransfer.PermitTransferFrom calldata permit,
 443 |     |         bytes32 witness,
 444 |     |         bytes calldata signature
 445 |     |     ) internal {
 446 |     |         uint256 beforeBalance = _paymentToken.balanceOf(address(this));
 447 |     |         ISignatureTransfer.SignatureTransferDetails memory details =
 448 |     |             ISignatureTransfer.SignatureTransferDetails({
 449 |     |                 to: address(this), requestedAmount: amount
 450 |     |             });
 451 |     |         permit2.permitWitnessTransferFrom(
 452 |     |             permit, details, owner, witness, BUY_WITNESS_TYPE_STRING, signature
 453 |     |         );
 454 |     |         uint256 received = _paymentToken.balanceOf(address(this)) - beforeBalance;
 455 |     |         if (received != amount) revert InexactTokenTransfer(amount, received);
 456 |     |     }
 457 |     | 
 458 |     |     function _prepareBuy(
 459 |     |         address buyer,
 460 |     |         uint256 outcomeId,
 461 |     |         uint256 desiredUnits,
 462 |     |         uint256 minUnits,
 463 |     |         uint256 maxPayment,
 464 |     |         uint64 deadline
 465 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 filledUnits) {
 466 |     |         if (buyer == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();
 467 |     |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.OPEN || block.timestamp >= closeAt) {
 468 |     |             revert MarketNotOpen();
 469 |     |         }
 470 |     |         if (emergencyController.isPaused(ProtocolTypes.PAUSE_PRIMARY_BUY)) {
 471 |     |             revert PauseActive(ProtocolTypes.PAUSE_PRIMARY_BUY);
 472 |     |         }
 473 |     |         if (block.timestamp > deadline) revert DeadlineExpired(deadline, block.timestamp);
 474 |     |         if (outcomeId >= outcomeCount) revert InvalidOutcome(outcomeId, outcomeCount);
 475 |     |         if (desiredUnits == 0) revert ZeroAmount();
 476 |     | 
 477 |     |         uint256 marketAvailable = uint256(marketPrimaryCap) - totalPrincipal;
 478 |     |         uint256 userAvailable = uint256(perUserPrimaryCap) - cumulativePrimaryBought[buyer];
 479 |     |         filledUnits = Math.min(desiredUnits, Math.min(marketAvailable, userAvailable));
 480 |     |         if (filledUnits < minUnits || filledUnits < minimumPrimaryUnits) {
 481 |     |             revert FillBelowMinimum(filledUnits, Math.max(minUnits, minimumPrimaryUnits));
 482 |     |         }
 483 |     |         if (filledUnits > maxPayment) revert PaymentAboveMaximum(filledUnits, maxPayment);
 484 |     |     }
 485 |     | 
 486 |     |     function _completeBuy(
 487 |     |         address buyer,
 488 |     |         uint256 outcomeId,
 489 |     |         uint256 desiredUnits,
 490 |     |         uint256 filledUnits
 491 |     |     ) internal {
 492 |     |         exposureGuard.reserve(filledUnits);
 493 |     |         firstBuyOccurred = true;
 494 |     |         totalPrincipal += filledUnits;
 495 |     |         principalByOutcome[outcomeId] += filledUnits;
 496 |     |         cumulativePrimaryBought[buyer] += filledUnits;
 497 |     | 
 498 |     |         uint8 weight = _earlyBirdWeight(block.timestamp);
 499 |     |         if (weight != 0) {
 500 |     |             uint256 score = filledUnits * weight;
 501 |     |             earlyBirdScore[buyer] += score;
 502 |     |             totalEarlyBirdScore += score;
 503 |     |         }
 504 |     | 
 505 |     |         _mint(buyer, outcomeId, filledUnits, "");
 506 |     |         emit PrimaryPurchased(
 507 |     |             buyer,
 508 |     |             outcomeId,
 509 |     |             desiredUnits,
 510 |     |             filledUnits,
 511 |     |             filledUnits,
 512 |     |             weight,
 513 |     |             cumulativePrimaryBought[buyer],
 514 |     |             totalPrincipal
 515 |     |         );
 516 |     |     }
 517 |     | 
 518 |     |     function _pullExact(address from, uint256 amount) internal {
 519 |     |         uint256 beforeBalance = _paymentToken.balanceOf(address(this));
 520 |     |         _paymentToken.safeTransferFrom(from, address(this), amount);
 521 |     |         uint256 received = _paymentToken.balanceOf(address(this)) - beforeBalance;
 522 |     |         if (received != amount) revert InexactTokenTransfer(amount, received);
 523 |     |     }
 524 |     | 
 525 |     |     function _earlyBirdWeight(uint256 timestamp) internal view returns (uint8) {
 526 |     |         if (!earlyBirdEnabled()) return 0;
 527 |     |         if (timestamp < earlyBirdStart) return 3;
 528 |     |         if (timestamp >= closeAt) return 0;
 529 |     |         uint256 duration = uint256(closeAt) - earlyBirdStart;
 530 |     |         uint256 offset = timestamp - earlyBirdStart;
 531 |     |         if (offset * 3 < duration) return 3;
 532 |     |         if (offset * 3 < duration * 2) return 2;
 533 |     |         return 1;
 534 |     |     }
 535 |     | 
 536 |     |     function resolve(uint256 outcomeId) external onlyCreator nonReentrant {
 537 |     |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.OPEN) revert MarketTerminal();
 538 |     |         if (block.timestamp < closeAt) revert MarketNotClosed();
 539 |     |         if (block.timestamp >= resolutionDeadline()) revert ResolutionWindowExpired();
 540 |     |         if (outcomeId >= outcomeCount) revert InvalidOutcome(outcomeId, outcomeCount);
 541 |     |         uint256 winningSupply = totalSupply(outcomeId);
 542 |     |         if (winningSupply == 0) revert WinningOutcomeHasNoSupply(outcomeId);
 543 |     | 
 544 |     |         ProtocolTypes.PayoutBreakdown memory breakdown = _calculatePayouts();
 545 |     |         marketState = ProtocolTypes.MarketState.RESOLVED;
 546 |     |         winningOutcome = outcomeId.toUint8();
 547 |     |         payoutBreakdown = breakdown;
 548 |     |         remainingWinningUnits = winningSupply;
 549 |     |         remainingWinnerPool = breakdown.winnerPool;
 550 |     |         remainingEarlyBirdScore = totalEarlyBirdScore;
 551 |     |         remainingEarlyBirdPool = breakdown.earlyBirdPool;
 552 |     | 
 553 |     |         uint256 fees = breakdown.protocolFee + breakdown.creatorFee;
 554 |     |         if (fees != 0) {
 555 |     |             _paymentToken.safeTransfer(address(feeVault), fees);
 556 |     |             feeVault.accrue(
 557 |     |                 _economics.protocolTreasury,
 558 |     |                 breakdown.protocolFee,
 559 |     |                 FEE_KIND_PROTOCOL_RAKE,
 560 |     |                 bytes32(uint256(uint160(address(this))))
 561 |     |             );
 562 |     |             feeVault.accrue(
 563 |     |                 creatorTreasury,
 564 |     |                 breakdown.creatorFee,
 565 |     |                 FEE_KIND_CREATOR_RAKE,
 566 |     |                 bytes32(uint256(uint160(address(this))))
 567 |     |             );
 568 |     |         }
 569 |     |         _assertCoverage();
 570 |     |         emit MarketResolved(
 571 |     |             outcomeId,
 572 |     |             breakdown.totalPrincipal,
 573 |     |             breakdown.totalRake,
 574 |     |             breakdown.protocolFee,
 575 |     |             breakdown.creatorFee,
 576 |     |             breakdown.earlyBirdPool,
 577 |     |             breakdown.winnerPool
 578 |     |         );
 579 |     |     }
 580 |     | 
 581 |     |     function creatorVoid() external onlyCreator {
 582 |     |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.OPEN) revert MarketTerminal();
 583 |     |         _void(ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_CREATOR);
 584 |     |     }
 585 |     | 
 586 |     |     function voidAfterDeadline() external {
 587 |     |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.OPEN) revert MarketTerminal();
 588 |     |         if (block.timestamp < resolutionDeadline()) revert TimeoutNotReached();
 589 |     |         _void(ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_TIMEOUT);
 590 |     |     }

 592 |     |     function _void(ProtocolTypes.MarketState terminalState) internal {
 593 |     |         marketState = terminalState;
 594 |     |         remainingRefundPrincipal = totalPrincipal;
 595 |     |         emit MarketVoided(terminalState, msg.sender, totalPrincipal);
 596 |     |     }

 598 |     |     function claimWinnings() external returns (uint256 payout) {
 599 |     |         return claimWinningsFor(msg.sender);
 600 |     |     }
 601 |     | 
 602 |     |     function claimWinningsFor(address owner) public nonReentrant returns (uint256 payout) {
 603 |     |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.RESOLVED) revert MarketNotClosed();
 604 |     |         _rejectProtocolMarketplaceOwner(owner);
 605 |     |         uint256 units = balanceOf(owner, winningOutcome);
 606 |     |         if (units == 0) revert NothingToClaim();
 607 |     |         payout = _consumeRemainingPool(
 608 |     |             units, remainingWinningUnits, remainingWinnerPool, keccak256("WINNER_POOL"), owner
 609 |     |         );
 610 |     |         remainingWinningUnits -= units;
 611 |     |         remainingWinnerPool -= payout;
 612 |     |         _burn(owner, winningOutcome, units);
 613 |     |         _paymentToken.safeTransfer(owner, payout);
 614 |     |         emit WinnerClaimed(owner, msg.sender, units, payout);
 615 |     |     }
 616 |     | 
 617 |     |     function claimEarlyBird() external returns (uint256 reward) {
 618 |     |         return claimEarlyBirdFor(msg.sender);
 619 |     |     }
 620 |     | 
 621 |     |     function claimEarlyBirdFor(address owner) public nonReentrant returns (uint256 reward) {
 622 |     |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.RESOLVED) revert MarketNotClosed();
 623 |     |         uint256 score = earlyBirdScore[owner];
 624 |     |         if (score == 0 || remainingEarlyBirdPool == 0) revert NothingToClaim();
 625 |     |         reward = _consumeRemainingPool(
 626 |     |             score,
 627 |     |             remainingEarlyBirdScore,
 628 |     |             remainingEarlyBirdPool,
 629 |     |             keccak256("EARLY_BIRD_POOL"),
 630 |     |             owner
 631 |     |         );
 632 |     |         earlyBirdScore[owner] = 0;
 633 |     |         remainingEarlyBirdScore -= score;
 634 |     |         remainingEarlyBirdPool -= reward;
 635 |     |         _paymentToken.safeTransfer(owner, reward);
 636 |     |         emit EarlyBirdClaimed(owner, msg.sender, score, reward);
 637 |     |     }

 639 |     |     function refund() external returns (uint256 amount) {
 640 |     |         return refundFor(msg.sender);
 641 |     |     }
 642 |     | 
 643 |     |     function refundFor(address owner) public nonReentrant returns (uint256 amount) {
 644 |     |         ProtocolTypes.MarketState state = marketState;
 645 |     |         if (
 646 |     |             state != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_CREATOR
 647 |     |                 && state != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_TIMEOUT
 648 |     |         ) revert MarketNotClosed();
 649 |     |         _rejectProtocolMarketplaceOwner(owner);
 650 |     | 
 651 |     |         for (uint256 outcomeId = 0; outcomeId < outcomeCount; ++outcomeId) {
 652 |     |             amount += balanceOf(owner, outcomeId);
 653 |     |         }
 654 |     |         if (amount == 0) revert NothingToClaim();
 655 |     |         remainingRefundPrincipal -= amount;
 656 |     |         if (state == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_TIMEOUT) {
 657 |     |             timeoutBonusUnits[owner] += amount;
 658 |     |         }
 659 |     |         for (uint256 outcomeId = 0; outcomeId < outcomeCount; ++outcomeId) {
 660 |     |             uint256 units = balanceOf(owner, outcomeId);
 661 |     |             if (units != 0) _burn(owner, outcomeId, units);
 662 |     |         }
 663 |     |         _paymentToken.safeTransfer(owner, amount);
 664 |     |         emit PrincipalRefunded(
 665 |     |             owner, msg.sender, amount, amount, state == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_TIMEOUT
 666 |     |         );
 667 |     |     }
 668 |     | 
 669 |     |     function fundTimeoutBonus(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {
 670 |     |         if (msg.sender != bondEscrow) revert Unauthorized(msg.sender);
 671 |     |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_TIMEOUT) revert MarketNotClosed();
 672 |     |         if (timeoutBonusFunded) revert AlreadySettled();
 673 |     |         if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();
 674 |     |         timeoutBonusFunded = true;
 675 |     |         remainingTimeoutBonusUnits = totalPrincipal;
 676 |     |         remainingTimeoutBonusPool = amount;
 677 |     |         // Bonus funding can increase terminal user liabilities above the last principal-only
 678 |     |         // report. Synchronize immediately; later claims may leave the report conservatively high.
 679 |     |         (uint256 previousGuardExposure, uint256 currentGuardExposure) =
 680 |     |             exposureGuard.sync(address(this));
 681 |     |         _assertCoverage();
 682 |     |         emit TimeoutBonusFunded(amount, totalPrincipal, previousGuardExposure, currentGuardExposure);
 683 |     |     }
 684 |     | 
 685 |     |     function claimTimeoutBonus() external returns (uint256 reward) {
 686 |     |         return claimTimeoutBonusFor(msg.sender);
 687 |     |     }
 688 |     | 
 689 |     |     function claimTimeoutBonusFor(address owner) public nonReentrant returns (uint256 reward) {
 690 |     |         if (!timeoutBonusFunded) revert NothingToClaim();
 691 |     |         uint256 units = timeoutBonusUnits[owner];
 692 |     |         if (units == 0) revert NothingToClaim();
 693 |     |         reward = _consumeRemainingPool(
 694 |     |             units,
 695 |     |             remainingTimeoutBonusUnits,
 696 |     |             remainingTimeoutBonusPool,
 697 |     |             keccak256("TIMEOUT_BONUS_POOL"),
 698 |     |             owner
 699 |     |         );
 700 |     |         timeoutBonusUnits[owner] = 0;
 701 |     |         remainingTimeoutBonusUnits -= units;
 702 |     |         remainingTimeoutBonusPool -= reward;
 703 |     |         _paymentToken.safeTransfer(owner, reward);
 704 |     |         emit TimeoutBonusClaimed(owner, msg.sender, units, reward);
 705 |     |     }
 706 |     | 
 707 |     |     function _rejectProtocolMarketplaceOwner(address owner) internal view {
 708 |     |         address protocolMarketplace = IMarketFactoryV1(factory).marketplace();
 709 |     |         if (protocolMarketplace != address(0) && owner == protocolMarketplace) {
 710 |     |             revert EscrowOwnerMustReturnListing(protocolMarketplace);
 711 |     |         }
 712 |     |     }
 713 |     | 
 714 |     |     function burnLosingPosition(uint256 outcomeId) external {
 715 |     |         if (marketState != ProtocolTypes.MarketState.RESOLVED) revert MarketNotClosed();
 716 |     |         if (outcomeId >= outcomeCount || outcomeId == winningOutcome) {
 717 |     |             revert InvalidOutcome(outcomeId, outcomeCount);
 718 |     |         }
 719 |     |         uint256 units = balanceOf(msg.sender, outcomeId);
 720 |     |         if (units == 0) revert NothingToClaim();
 721 |     |         _burn(msg.sender, outcomeId, units);
 722 |     |         emit LosingPositionBurned(msg.sender, outcomeId, units);
 723 |     |     }
 724 |     | 
 725 |     |     function _calculatePayouts()
 726 |     |         internal
 727 |     |         view
 728 |     |         returns (ProtocolTypes.PayoutBreakdown memory result)
 729 |     |     {
 730 |     |         uint256 principal = totalPrincipal;
 731 |     |         uint256 rake = Math.mulDiv(principal, _economics.creatorRakeBps, ProtocolTypes.BPS);
 732 |     |         uint256 protocolFee = Math.mulDiv(rake, _economics.protocolShareBps, ProtocolTypes.BPS);
 733 |     |         uint256 creatorNetRake = rake - protocolFee;
 734 |     |         uint256 earlyPool = earlyBirdEnabled() && totalEarlyBirdScore != 0
 735 |     |             ? Math.mulDiv(creatorNetRake, _economics.earlyBirdShareBps, ProtocolTypes.BPS)
 736 |     |             : 0;
 737 |     |         result = ProtocolTypes.PayoutBreakdown({
 738 |     |             totalPrincipal: principal,
 739 |     |             totalRake: rake,
 740 |     |             protocolFee: protocolFee,
 741 |     |             earlyBirdPool: earlyPool,
 742 |     |             creatorFee: creatorNetRake - earlyPool,
 743 |     |             winnerPool: principal - rake
 744 |     |         });
 745 |     |     }
 746 |     | 
 747 |     |     function _consumeRemainingPool(
 748 |     |         uint256 units,
 749 |     |         uint256 remainingUnits,
 750 |     |         uint256 remainingPool,
 751 |     |         bytes32 poolId,
 752 |     |         address owner
 753 |     |     ) internal returns (uint256 amount) {
 754 |     |         if (units == 0 || remainingUnits == 0 || units > remainingUnits) {
 755 |     |             revert NothingToClaim();
 756 |     |         }
 757 |     |         if (units == remainingUnits) {
 758 |     |             amount = remainingPool;
 759 |     |             emit FinalRemainderAssigned(poolId, owner, amount);
 760 |     |         } else {
 761 |     |             amount = Math.mulDiv(units, remainingPool, remainingUnits);
 762 |     |         }
 763 |     |     }
 764 |     | 
 765 |     |     function _assertCoverage() internal view {
 766 |     |         uint256 liabilities;
 767 |     |         if (marketState == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.RESOLVED) {
 768 |     |             liabilities = remainingWinnerPool + remainingEarlyBirdPool;
 769 |     |         } else if (
 770 |     |             marketState == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_CREATOR
 771 |     |                 || marketState == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.VOIDED_TIMEOUT
 772 |     |         ) {
 773 |     |             liabilities = remainingRefundPrincipal + remainingTimeoutBonusPool;
 774 |     |         } else {
 775 |     |             liabilities = totalPrincipal;
 776 |     |         }
 777 |     |         uint256 balance = _paymentToken.balanceOf(address(this));
 778 |     |         if (balance < liabilities) revert Insolvent(balance, liabilities);
 779 |     |     }
 780 |     | 
 781 | *   |     function _validateTimes(uint64 creationTime, uint64 marketClose, uint64 earlyStart)
 782 |     |         internal
 783 |     |         pure
 784 |     |     {
 785 | *   |         if (
 786 | *   |             marketClose < creationTime + 5 minutes || marketClose > creationTime + 90 days
 787 | *   |                 || earlyStart < creationTime || earlyStart >= marketClose
 788 |     |         ) revert InvalidConfiguration("market.times");
 789 |     |     }
 790 |     | }

  11 |     | import { BondEscrowV1 } from "../../src/core/BondEscrowV1.sol";

  19 |     | /// @dev A bounded actor. Echidna/Medusa target only the harness, so actor calls must originate
  20 |     | /// from the harness and cannot be forged by fuzz senders.
  21 |     | contract SecurityActor is ERC1155Holder {
  22 |     |     address internal immutable harness;
  23 |     |     MockUSDC internal immutable usdc;
  24 |     |     MarketVaultCoreV1 internal immutable market;
  25 |     | 
  26 |     |     constructor(address harness_, MockUSDC usdc_, MarketVaultCoreV1 market_) {
  27 |     |         harness = harness_;
  28 |     |         usdc = usdc_;
  29 |     |         market = market_;
  30 |     |         IERC20(address(usdc_)).approve(address(market_), type(uint256).max);
  31 |     |     }
  32 |     | 
  33 |     |     modifier onlyHarness() {
  34 |     |         require(msg.sender == harness);
  35 |     |         _;
  36 |     |     }
  37 |     | 
  38 |     |     function buy(uint256 outcome, uint256 amount) external onlyHarness {
  39 |     |         market.buy(outcome, amount, amount, amount, uint64(block.timestamp + 1 days));
  40 |     |     }
  41 |     | 
  42 |     |     function transferTo(SecurityActor recipient, uint256 outcome, uint256 amount)
  43 |     |         external
  44 |     |         onlyHarness
  45 |     |     {
  46 |     |         market.safeTransferFrom(address(this), address(recipient), outcome, amount, "");
  47 |     |     }
  48 |     | 
  49 |     |     function refund() external onlyHarness {
  50 |     |         market.refundFor(address(this));
  51 |     |     }
  52 |     | 
  53 |     |     function claimWinner() external onlyHarness {
  54 |     |         market.claimWinningsFor(address(this));
  55 |     |     }
  56 |     | 
  57 |     |     function claimEarlyBird() external onlyHarness {
  58 |     |         market.claimEarlyBirdFor(address(this));
  59 |     |     }
  60 |     | 
  61 |     |     function claimTimeoutBonus() external onlyHarness {
  62 |     |         market.claimTimeoutBonusFor(address(this));
  63 |     |     }
  64 |     | 
  65 |     |     function burnLosing(uint256 outcome) external onlyHarness {
  66 |     |         market.burnLosingPosition(outcome);
  67 |     |     }
  68 |     | }
  69 |     | 
  70 |     | /// @notice Stateful accounting campaign shared by Echidna and Medusa.
  71 |     | /// @dev All actions are bounded and expected reverts are swallowed to keep campaigns exploring.
  72 | *   | contract EchidnaMarketAccounting {
  73 |     |     MockUSDC public immutable usdc;
  74 |     |     LaunchExposureGuardV1 public immutable guard;
  75 |     |     FeeVaultV1 public immutable feeVault;
  76 |     |     BondEscrowV1 public immutable bondEscrow;
  77 |     |     MarketVaultCoreV1 public immutable market;
  78 |     | 
  79 |     |     SecurityActor[4] internal actors;
  80 |     | 
  81 |     |     constructor() {
  82 | *   |         usdc = new MockUSDC();
  83 |     |         ProtocolConfigV1 config =
  84 | *   |             new ProtocolConfigV1(address(this), address(usdc), address(0xFEE));
  85 |     |         EmergencyControllerV1 emergency =
  86 | *   |             new EmergencyControllerV1(address(this), address(0xE911));
  87 | *   |         guard = new LaunchExposureGuardV1(address(this), 50_000e6);
  88 | *   |         feeVault = new FeeVaultV1(address(this), address(usdc));
  89 | *   |         bondEscrow = new BondEscrowV1(address(this), address(usdc));
  90 | *   |         CloneMarketVaultV1 cloneImplementation = new CloneMarketVaultV1();
  91 | *   |         FullMarketDeployerV1 fullDeployer = new FullMarketDeployerV1(address(this));
  92 | *   |         MarketFactoryV1 factory = new MarketFactoryV1(
  93 |     |             address(this),
  94 |     |             address(config),
  95 |     |             address(emergency),
  96 |     |             address(guard),
  97 |     |             address(bondEscrow),
  98 |     |             address(feeVault),
  99 |     |             address(fullDeployer),
 100 |     |             address(cloneImplementation),
 101 |     |             address(0)
 102 |     |         );
 103 | *   |         guard.setFactory(address(factory));
 104 | *   |         feeVault.setFactory(address(factory));
 105 | *   |         bondEscrow.setFactory(address(factory));
 106 | *   |         fullDeployer.setFactory(address(factory));
 107 |     | 
 108 | *   |         usdc.mint(address(this), 100e6);
 109 | *   |         usdc.approve(address(factory), type(uint256).max);
 110 | *   |         ProtocolTypes.CreateMarketParams memory params = ProtocolTypes.CreateMarketParams({
 111 | *   |             rulesHash: keccak256("security-campaign-rules"),
 112 |     |             metadataURI: "ipfs://security/{id}.json",
 113 |     |             resolutionSourceHash: bytes32(0),
 114 |     |             resolutionSourceURI: "",
 115 | *   |             outcomeCount: 3,
 116 | *   |             closeAt: uint64(block.timestamp + 5 minutes),
 117 |     |             earlyBirdStart: uint64(block.timestamp),
 118 |     |             creatorTreasury: address(this),
 119 |     |             deploymentMode: ProtocolTypes.DeploymentMode.CLONE,
 120 |     |             featureFlags: ProtocolTypes.FEATURE_EARLY_BIRD,
 121 | *   |             creatorRakeBps: 500,
 122 |     |             creatorC2CFeeBps: 0,
 123 |     |             perUserPrimaryCap: 100e6,
 124 | *   |             marketPrimaryCap: 500e6,
 125 | *   |             minimumPrimaryUnits: 10_000,
 126 |     |             minimumC2CUnits: 10_000,
 127 | *   |             creatorBond: 10e6
 128 |     |         });
 129 | *   |         market = MarketVaultCoreV1(factory.createMarket(params, bytes32("security-campaign")));
 130 |     | 
 131 | *   |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < actors.length; ++i) {
 132 | *   |             actors[i] = new SecurityActor(address(this), usdc, market);
 133 | *   |             usdc.mint(address(actors[i]), 1_000e6);
 134 |     |         }
 135 |     |     }
 136 |     | 
 137 |     |     function buy(uint256 actorSeed, uint256 outcomeSeed, uint256 amountSeed) external {
 138 |     |         if (market.isTerminal() || block.timestamp >= market.closeAt()) return;
 139 |     |         SecurityActor actor = actors[actorSeed % actors.length];
 140 |     |         uint256 marketAvailable = market.marketPrimaryCap() - market.totalPrincipal();
 141 |     |         uint256 userAvailable =
 142 |     |             market.perUserPrimaryCap() - market.cumulativePrimaryBought(address(actor));
 143 |     |         uint256 available = marketAvailable < userAvailable ? marketAvailable : userAvailable;
 144 |     |         uint256 minimum = market.minimumPrimaryUnits();
 145 |     |         if (available < minimum) return;
 146 |     |         uint256 amount = minimum + (amountSeed % (available - minimum + 1));
 147 |     |         try actor.buy(outcomeSeed % market.outcomeCount(), amount) { } catch { }
 148 |     |     }
 149 |     | 
 150 |     |     function transfer(
 151 |     |         uint256 fromSeed,
 152 |     |         uint256 toSeed,
 153 |     |         uint256 outcomeSeed,
 154 |     |         uint256 amountSeed
 155 |     |     ) external {
 156 |     |         uint256 fromIndex = fromSeed % actors.length;
 157 |     |         uint256 toIndex = toSeed % actors.length;
 158 |     |         if (fromIndex == toIndex) return;
 159 |     |         uint256 outcome = outcomeSeed % market.outcomeCount();
 160 |     |         uint256 balance = market.balanceOf(address(actors[fromIndex]), outcome);
 161 |     |         if (balance == 0) return;
 162 |     |         uint256 amount = 1 + (amountSeed % balance);
 163 |     |         try actors[fromIndex].transferTo(actors[toIndex], outcome, amount) { } catch { }
 164 |     |     }
 165 |     | 
 166 |     |     function creatorResolve(uint256 outcomeSeed) external {
 167 |     |         if (market.isTerminal()) return;
 168 |     |         if (block.timestamp < market.closeAt() || block.timestamp >= market.resolutionDeadline()) {
 169 |     |             return;
 170 |     |         }
 171 |     |         uint256 outcome = outcomeSeed % market.outcomeCount();
 172 |     |         if (market.totalSupply(outcome) == 0) return;
 173 |     |         try market.resolve(outcome) { } catch { }
 174 |     |     }
 175 |     | 
 176 |     |     function creatorVoid() external {
 177 |     |         if (market.isTerminal()) return;
 178 |     |         try market.creatorVoid() { } catch { }
 179 |     |     }
 180 |     | 
 181 |     |     function timeoutVoid() external {
 182 |     |         if (market.isTerminal() || block.timestamp < market.resolutionDeadline()) return;
 183 |     |         try market.voidAfterDeadline() { } catch { }
 184 |     |     }
 185 |     | 
 186 |     |     function settleBond() external {
 187 |     |         if (!market.isTerminal()) return;
 188 |     |         try bondEscrow.settleBond(address(market)) { } catch { }
 189 |     |     }
 190 |     | 
 191 |     |     function refund(uint256 actorSeed) external {
 192 |     |         SecurityActor actor = actors[actorSeed % actors.length];
 193 |     |         try actor.refund() { } catch { }
 194 |     |     }
 195 |     | 
 196 |     |     function claimWinner(uint256 actorSeed) external {
 197 |     |         SecurityActor actor = actors[actorSeed % actors.length];
 198 |     |         try actor.claimWinner() { } catch { }
 199 |     |     }
 200 |     | 
 201 |     |     function claimEarlyBird(uint256 actorSeed) external {
 202 |     |         SecurityActor actor = actors[actorSeed % actors.length];
 203 |     |         try actor.claimEarlyBird() { } catch { }
 204 |     |     }
 205 |     | 
 206 |     |     function claimTimeoutBonus(uint256 actorSeed) external {
 207 |     |         SecurityActor actor = actors[actorSeed % actors.length];
 208 |     |         try actor.claimTimeoutBonus() { } catch { }
 209 |     |     }
 210 |     | 
 211 |     |     function burnLosing(uint256 actorSeed, uint256 outcomeSeed) external {
 212 |     |         SecurityActor actor = actors[actorSeed % actors.length];
 213 |     |         try actor.burnLosing(outcomeSeed % market.outcomeCount()) { } catch { }
 214 |     |     }
 215 |     | 
 216 |     |     function syncGuard() external {
 217 |     |         try guard.sync(address(market)) { } catch { }
 218 |     |     }
 219 |     | 
 220 |     |     function claimCreatorBond() external {
 221 |     |         try bondEscrow.claimFor(address(this)) { } catch { }
 222 |     |     }
 223 |     | 
 224 |     |     function claimCreatorFee() external {
 225 |     |         try feeVault.claimFor(address(this)) { } catch { }
 226 |     |     }
 227 |     | 
 228 |     |     function _vaultSolvent() internal view returns (bool) {
 229 |     |         uint256 liabilities;
 230 |     |         ProtocolTypes.MarketState state = market.marketState();
 231 |     |         if (state == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.OPEN) {
 232 |     |             liabilities = market.totalPrincipal();
 233 |     |         } else if (state == ProtocolTypes.MarketState.RESOLVED) {
 234 |     |             liabilities = market.remainingWinnerPool() + market.remainingEarlyBirdPool();
 235 |     |         } else {
 236 |     |             liabilities = market.remainingRefundPrincipal() + market.remainingTimeoutBonusPool();
 237 |     |         }
 238 |     |         return usdc.balanceOf(address(market)) >= liabilities;
 239 |     |     }
 240 |     | 
 241 |     |     function _supplyConsistent() internal view returns (bool) {
 242 |     |         uint256 summedSupply;
 243 |     |         for (uint256 outcome = 0; outcome < market.outcomeCount(); ++outcome) {
 244 |     |             summedSupply += market.totalSupply(outcome);
 245 |     |         }
 246 |     |         return summedSupply == market.totalSupply() && summedSupply <= market.totalPrincipal();
 247 |     |     }
 248 |     | 
 249 |     |     function _guardConservative() internal view returns (bool) {
 250 |     |         return guard.reportedExposure(address(market)) >= market.guardExposure()
 251 |     |             && guard.totalReportedExposure() >= guard.reportedExposure(address(market));
 252 |     |     }
 253 |     | 
 254 |     |     function _segregatedVaultsSolvent() internal view returns (bool) {
 255 |     |         return usdc.balanceOf(address(feeVault)) >= feeVault.totalCredits()
 256 |     |             && usdc.balanceOf(address(bondEscrow))
 257 |     |                 >= bondEscrow.totalLocked() + bondEscrow.totalCredits();
 258 |     |     }
 259 |     | 
 260 |     |     function echidna_vault_assets_cover_liabilities() external view returns (bool) {
 261 |     |         return _vaultSolvent();
 262 |     |     }
 263 |     | 
 264 |     |     function echidna_supply_is_conserved() external view returns (bool) {
 265 |     |         return _supplyConsistent();
 266 |     |     }
 267 |     | 
 268 |     |     function echidna_guard_is_conservative() external view returns (bool) {
 269 |     |         return _guardConservative();
 270 |     |     }
 271 |     | 
 272 |     |     function echidna_fee_and_bond_vaults_are_solvent() external view returns (bool) {
 273 |     |         return _segregatedVaultsSolvent();
 274 |     |     }
 275 |     | 
 276 |     |     function property_vault_assets_cover_liabilities() external view returns (bool) {
 277 |     |         return _vaultSolvent();
 278 |     |     }
 279 |     | 
 280 |     |     function property_supply_is_conserved() external view returns (bool) {
 281 |     |         return _supplyConsistent();
 282 |     |     }
 283 |     | 
 284 |     |     function property_guard_is_conservative() external view returns (bool) {
 285 |     |         return _guardConservative();
 286 |     |     }
 287 |     | 
 288 |     |     function property_fee_and_bond_vaults_are_solvent() external view returns (bool) {
 289 |     |         return _segregatedVaultsSolvent();
 290 |     |     }
